Theorem 216.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a geochemistry algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 946. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 607/2.

By the convergence analysis framework applied to geochemistry structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 946/607 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
